+function normalizeEvidenceLayoutValue(value: any) {
+  const text = String(value || "").trim().toLowerCase();
+
+  if (!text || text === "auto") return "GRID_2X2";
+  if (text === "grid-2x2" || text === "grid_2x2") return "GRID_2X2";
+  if (text === "two-per-page" || text === "two_per_page" || text === "two-columns" || text === "two_columns") return "TWO_PER_PAGE";
+  if (text === "one-per-page" || text === "one_per_page") return "ONE_PER_PAGE";
+  if (text === "attachment-list" || text === "attachment_list") return "ATTACHMENT_LIST";
+  if (text === "stacked") return "STACKED";
+  if (text === "compact") return "COMPACT";
+
+  return value || "GRID_2X2";
+}
